# Well Beyond Dental

## About **Well Beyond Dental**

At Well Beyond Dental, we are truly your one-stop-shop for Reno dental care. We are a team of on-site dentistry specialists who each bring a unique set of expertise to our patients. That means every aspect of your oral health can be provided for in our comfortable, convenient office.

Our team of dental experts offers the most advanced dentistry services with a superior quality of care that exceeds the highest patient expectations. From dental implants to sleep apnea therapy and TMJ treatment, our dentist team does it all. Our Reno dental office meets all of your oral health care needs.

## Revitalize Your Smile  
**in One Day!**

Say goodbye to multiple dental appointments and hello to a radiant, confident smile achieved in just one visit! At our practice, we take pride in offering fast smile dental solutions that combine efficiency with exceptional results. With our state-of-the-art technology and expertise, you can experience life-changing treatments like same day dental crowns, which restore the strength and beauty of your teeth, or same day teeth whitening to enhance your smile’s brilliance in record time.

Our innovative one day smile makeovers ensure you leave our office with a transformed appearance and renewed confidence. Whether you’re looking to improve the function or aesthetics of your teeth, our comprehensive same-day services deliver high-quality care and stunning results—all tailored to meet your unique dental needs. Experience the convenience and excellence of our one-visit smile transformations!

[Fix Your Smile In a Day!](https://wellbeyonddental.com/same-day-cosmetic-dentistry/)

## Get The Smile You  
**DESERVE!**

Well Beyond Dental is now offering Invisalign clear aligners! Now is the time to get the smile you DESERVE.  Contact us today and find out why you’ll choose Invisalign vs. braces every time!

[Call us Today](tel:7757863472)

## Featured Services

### General Dentistry

We are the best general dentist office in Reno to prevent, evaluate, diagnose and treat dental problems as early as possible to ensure the best outcome.

[Learn More About General Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/general-dentistry/)

### Restorative Dentistry

We help maintain the functional healthiness of your smile and will take the steps necessary to get your teeth back to feeling good as new.

[Learn More About Restorative Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/restorative-dentistry/)

### Cosmetic Dentistry

From subtle changes to major repairs, Well Beyond Dental perform a variety of cosmetic dentistry services like implants, veneers & teeth whitening.

[Learn More About Cosmetic Dentistry Service](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/cosmetic-dentistry/)

### Emergency Dental Services

Dental emergencies can be stressful and painful, which is why we offer same-day appointments and prioritize your comfort and well-being.

[Learn More About Emergency Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/emergency-services/)

### Cold Laser Therapy

Our Reno dentists use cold laser therapy to stimulate healing while using low levels of lasers to treat inflammation and heal ulcerations.

[Learn More About Cold Laser Therapy](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/cold-laser-therapy/)

### TMJ Treatment

At Well Beyond we us state-of-the-art 3-D imaging to accurately identify the root cause of your pain by providing TMJ treatment services.

[Learn More About TMJ Relief](https://tmjsleepnevada.com/)

[ALL SERVICES](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/)

# Emergency Dentist in Reno, NV

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Well Beyond Dental provides emergency dental services to patients in need. Whether you’re experiencing severe tooth pain, a broken tooth, or any other dental emergency, our experienced and caring team is here to help. We understand that dental emergencies can be stressful and painful, which is why we offer same-day appointments and prioritize your comfort and well-being. From root canals to extractions, we have the expertise to provide the care you need to get back to feeling your best.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Well Beyond Emergency Services in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Well-Beyond-Emergency-Services.jpg "Well Beyond Emergency Services")

## Emergency Dental Services in Reno, NV

![Dental Emergency in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Dental-Emergency.png "Dental Emergency in Reno")

![Emergency Dentist in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Well-Beyond-Emergency.jpg "Emergency Dentist in Reno")

##### Toothache

A toothache can be a dental emergency if the pain is severe and persistent, and is often an indication of an underlying dental issue. A toothache can be caused by a number of things, including tooth decay, gum disease, a cracked tooth, or an abscess. If left untreated, a toothache can worsen and lead to more serious dental problems, such as infection or tooth loss.

If you are experiencing a severe toothache, it’s important to seek emergency dental care as soon as possible. Well Beyond Dental can diagnose the underlying cause of the toothache and provide treatment to alleviate the pain and prevent further damage. Treatment may include a filling, root canal, or tooth extraction, depending on the severity of the toothache and the underlying condition.

Ignoring a toothache can lead to more serious dental problems and may require more extensive and costly treatment in the future. So, if you have a toothache, don’t delay seeking emergency dental care.

##### Broken or Chipped Tooth

A broken or chipped tooth can be considered a dental emergency, especially if it is causing pain, discomfort, or affecting your ability to eat or speak. A broken or chipped tooth can occur due to trauma or injury, biting down on hard foods, or as a result of tooth decay.

If you have a broken or chipped tooth, it’s important to seek emergency dental care as soon as possible. The experts at Well Beyond Dental can evaluate the severity of the damage and provide treatment to restore the tooth. Treatment may include bonding, a filling, a crown, or veneer, depending on the extent of the damage.

Ignoring a broken or chipped tooth can lead to further damage or infection, which may require more extensive and costly treatment in the future. It’s important to address the issue promptly to prevent complications and preserve the integrity of your teeth. So, if you have a broken or chipped tooth, contact us to schedule an emergency dental appointment.

##### Lost Filling or Crown

Losing a filling or crown means the affected tooth may be vulnerable to further damage or infection. A filling or crown can become dislodged due to injury, tooth decay, or wear and tear over time.

If you lose a filling or crown, it’s important to seek emergency dental care as soon as possible. The Well Beyond Dental team can evaluate the damage and provide treatment to restore the tooth. Depending on the extent of the damage, treatment may involve replacing the filling or crown, performing a root canal, or even extracting the tooth if it cannot be saved.

Ignoring a lost filling or crown can lead to further damage or infection, which may require more extensive and costly treatment in the future. It’s important to address the issue promptly to prevent complications and preserve the integrity of your teeth. So, if you lose a filling or crown, contact Well Beyond Dental to schedule an emergency dental appointment.

##### Abscess

An abscess is a dental emergency that requires immediate attention from a dentist. An abscess is a pocket of pus that forms in the tooth or gums due to a bacterial infection. It can cause severe pain, swelling, and fever, and if left untreated, it can spread to other parts of the body and cause serious health problems.

If you suspect you have an abscess, it’s important to seek emergency dental care as soon as possible. Well beyond Dental can diagnose the abscess and provide treatment to alleviate the pain and prevent the infection from spreading. Treatment may include draining the abscess, prescribing antibiotics, performing a root canal, or even extracting the affected tooth.

Ignoring an abscess can lead to more serious dental and medical problems, including sepsis, which is a life-threatening condition. So, if you suspect you have an abscess, don’t delay seeking emergency dental care from Well Beyond Dental.

# Our Services

Well Beyond Dental in Reno is dedicated to providing quality dental care for the whole family. Their experienced team of dentists and hygienists offer a wide range of services, from routine check-ups and cleanings to more complex procedures.

[CONTACT US TODAY](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Our Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Our-Services.jpeg "Our Services")

Our Services

## The Best Dental Care in Reno!

![WBD General Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-General-Dentistry.jpg "WBD General Dentistry")

### General Dentistry

Our dental services are backed by years of experience and the latest technology available. We strive to take our general dentistry treatments in Reno beyond other practices. Regular checkups are vital to maintaining good oral health.

[General Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/general-dentistry/)

![Cosmetic Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-X-Ray.jpg "Cosmetic Dentistry")

### Cosmetic Dentistry

Well Beyond Dental offers cosmetic dentistry services in Reno where work can be done in order to improve the appearance of the teeth or gums. We offer implant restorations, veneers, and teeth whitening as cosmetic dental solutions.

[Cosmetic Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/cosmetic-dentistry/)

![WBD Restorative Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Work.jpg "WBD Restorative Dentistry")

### Restorative Dentistry

Restorative dentistry refers to the steps taken by your Reno dentist to restore damaged teeth, and get your oral hygiene back to being healthy. These procedures are meant to rehabilitate and give you back the functionality of your teeth.

[Restorative Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/restorative-dentistry/)

![WBD Preventative Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/02/Smiling-Dental-Patient.jpg "Preventative Dentistry")

### Preventative Dentistry

The proactive steps taken by your Reno dentist to maintain healthy teeth and gums, preventing dental issues before they arise. These practices are designed to protect your oral health and ensure the long-term functionality of your teeth.

[Preventative Dentistry Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/preventative-dentistry/)

## Other Services

### Cold Laser Therapy

Treat inflammation of the tissues and heal ulcerations. Cold laser therapy promotes healing of the sensitive area through photobiomodulation (PBM) which is a form of light therapy. PBM is very effective after longer dental treatments or if you have inflammation in your mouth or jaw joints.

[Cold Laser Treatment](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/cold-laser-therapy/)

### Emergency Dental Care

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand that dental emergencies can happen at any time. That’s why we offer emergency dental treatment for our patients 7 days a week. Whether you’re experiencing a toothache, a chipped tooth, or a lost filling, our team of experienced dentists will be able to provide you with the care you need.

[Emergency Dentist Services](tel:7757863472)

![Sleep Doctor in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/TMJ_SleepApneaBreathing_image_ad_600x600_vD.jpg "Sleep Doctor in Reno")

## Sleep Disorder & TMJ Specialized Care Please Visit Our Sleep Therapy Centre

[VISIT OUR TMJ & SLEEP THERAPY CENTRE](https://tmjsleepnevada.com/)

# General Dentistry Services in Reno

Well Beyond Dental is Reno’s premiere choice for General Dental Services. Our highly trained and experienced staff provides the highest quality of care possible. We offer a full range of services, from preventive care to complex restorative treatments. Our goal is to help our patients achieve and maintain optimum oral health.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![General Dental Care in Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/great-smile.jpg "General Dental Care")

## Professional General Dentist in Reno, NV

![General Dentistry Services in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Well-Beyond_Still_3.jpg "General Dentistry Services in Reno")

![General Dental Care in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/gallery_home019-1.jpeg "General Dental Care")

##### Teeth Cleanings

Teeth cleaning is a procedure for the removal of tartar (mineralized plaque) that may develop even with careful brushing and flossing, especially in areas that are difficult to reach in routine toothbrushing. Professional cleaning includes tooth scaling and tooth polishing and debridement if too much tartar has accumulated. This involves the use of various instruments or devices to loosen and remove deposits from the teeth.

Dental cleanings should be performed by your Reno dental hygienist about every 6 months. Routine general dentistry visits help maintain good oral hygiene and also keep plaque down. They also help avoid future problems such as tooth decay, and will keep your teeth and gums healthy.

##### Dental Exams

Dental exams help give your dentist insight into your oral hygiene. Here, a Reno NV dentist will check for cavities and gum disease and evaluate your risk of developing other oral problems. A dental exam is used to detect oral health problems, and gives your dentist the chance to further explain the different methods for caring for your teeth.

Visiting the dentist on a regular basis allows your dentist to not only ensure your teeth are healthy but to also look for warning signs of any additional health problems. Research shows that there is a correlation between poor oral health and conditions such as diabetes, cardiovascular disease, stroke, and premature or low birth rate. It’s important to remember that your oral health can have a big impact on your overall health.

##### Digital X-Rays

Our Reno general dentistry office utilizes digital photo technology that provides our dentists with full visibility of the structures of your teeth. They show details of the teeth that can be helpful when detecting oral problems, such as cracks, and are able to show you potential problem areas. Digital photos allow our patients to be more informed and better prepared for future appointments with a dentist in Reno NV.

##### 3-D Dental Imaging

3D dental imaging is slowly becoming the new standard of care in dental practices. 3D dental scanning allows dentists to digitally reconstruct your teeth and skull in 3D. Dental practitioners can interact with this 3D model and visualize aspects of the model that would otherwise be difficult or impossible to access. This new perspective allows clinicians to more effectively diagnose an issue or provide a more effective means of treatment.

# 3D Dental Imaging

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we pride ourselves on offering state-of-the-art dental services to ensure the highest quality care for our patients. Our 3D Dental Imaging service is a testament to our commitment to advanced technology and personalized healthcare.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Dental Image Review](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/digital-image-review.jpg "Dental Image Review")

## About 3D Dental Imaging

![Dental Analysis](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Dental-Analysis.jpg "Dental Analysis")

![Dental Patient](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-General-Dentistry.jpg "Dental Patient")

##### What is 3D Dental Imaging?

3D Dental Imaging, also known as Cone Beam Computed Tomography (CBCT), is a cutting-edge diagnostic tool that provides detailed three-dimensional images of your oral and maxillofacial structures.

Unlike traditional dental X-rays, 3D Dental Imaging captures a comprehensive view of your teeth, jaw, and surrounding tissues in a single scan.

##### Why Choose 3D Dental Imaging?

**Enhanced Diagnostic Accuracy:** Unlike traditional X-rays, 3D Dental Imaging provides a three-dimensional view, enabling our dental professionals to diagnose issues with unparalleled accuracy. This comprehensive insight allows for more precise treatment planning.

**Minimized Radiation Exposure:** Our state-of-the-art 3D Dental Imaging technology is designed to minimize radiation exposure while still capturing detailed images. Your safety is our priority, and we strive to ensure that you receive the highest standard of care with the lowest possible radiation dose.

**Comprehensive Treatment Planning:** Whether you’re considering dental implants, orthodontic treatment, or addressing complex dental issues, 3D Dental Imaging helps us create a detailed treatment plan tailored to your unique needs. This technology is an invaluable tool in optimizing the success of various dental procedures.

**Efficient and Time-Saving:** 3D Dental Imaging allows for quicker and more efficient appointments. The detailed images obtained help streamline the diagnosis and treatment process, leading to a more comfortable experience for you.

##### Schedule Your Appointment Today

Investing in your oral health starts with choosing the right dental imaging technology. Schedule an appointment with Well Beyond Dental today to experience the benefits of 3D Dental Imaging firsthand. Our experienced team is dedicated to providing personalized care that goes well beyond your expectations.

Choose advanced dental care – choose 3D Dental Imaging at Well Beyond Dental. Your smile deserves the best.

# Dental Exams

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand that your oral health plays a vital role in your overall well-being. Regular dental exams are an essential part of maintaining a healthy smile and preventing potential oral health issues. Our experienced team is committed to providing comprehensive dental care, and we believe that educating our patients about the importance of dental exams is crucial for their oral health journey.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![WBD Dental Appointment](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Appointment.jpg "WBD Dental Appointment")

## Thorough Dental Exams in Reno, NV

![Dental Exam Tools](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Exam.jpg "Dental Exam Tools")

![Dental Exam Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Work.jpg "Dental Exam Services")

##### What is a Dental Exam?

A dental exam, also known as a check-up or dental check, is a thorough evaluation of your oral health conducted by a qualified dentist. It involves an examination of your teeth, gums, jaw, and other oral structures. During the exam, your dentist will assess the condition of your teeth, detect any existing or potential issues, and recommend appropriate treatments or preventive measures.

##### Why Are Dental Exams Important?

**Early Detection of Oral Health Issues:** Dental exams allow your dentist to identify dental problems in their early stages, such as tooth decay, gum disease, oral infections, and oral cancer. Detecting these issues early can help prevent further damage and minimize the need for extensive treatments.

**Prevention is Key:** Prevention is always better than cure when it comes to oral health. Regular dental exams enable your dentist to identify and address risk factors that could lead to dental problems, such as plaque buildup, tooth grinding, improper brushing techniques, or poor nutrition. By taking preventive measures, such as dental cleanings, fluoride treatments, or personalized oral hygiene instructions, we can help you maintain optimal oral health.

**Maintain Oral Hygiene:** Dental exams provide an opportunity for your dentist to assess your oral hygiene practices and offer guidance on proper brushing, flossing, and other oral care techniques. They can answer any questions you may have and recommend suitable oral care products to help you maintain a healthy smile between visits.

**Individualized Treatment Plans:** Every individual has unique dental needs and concerns. Dental exams allow your dentist to create personalized treatment plans tailored to your specific requirements. Whether it’s restorative treatments like fillings or crowns, orthodontic solutions, or cosmetic procedures, your dentist will provide recommendations based on your oral health goals.

**Overall Health Connection:** Oral health is closely linked to overall health. Many systemic conditions, including diabetes, cardiovascular disease, and certain cancers, have oral manifestations that can be detected during a dental exam. Regular exams can help identify these signs early, leading to early intervention and improved overall health outcomes.

**Dental Anxiety Management:** Dental anxiety is a common concern among patients. Regular dental exams can help build trust and rapport between you and your dentist. By consistently visiting our practice, you’ll become more familiar with our team and surroundings, helping to alleviate anxiety and ensuring a more comfortable dental experience.

##### Schedule Your Dental Exam Today

We prioritize your oral health and strive to provide exceptional care to all our patients. We encourage you to schedule regular dental exams, as prevention is the key to maintaining a healthy smile. Our skilled dental professionals are dedicated to helping you achieve optimal oral health and providing you with a positive dental experience.

Contact us today to book your dental exam or if you have any questions about our services. We look forward to partnering with you on your journey to a healthy and beautiful smile.

# Digital X-Rays

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental,  we prioritize your oral health by integrating cutting-edge technology into our services. One such advancement that sets us apart is our Digital X-Rays service. In this modern era, we believe in providing our patients with the most efficient and effective diagnostic tools, ensuring a comprehensive approach to your dental care.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Dental X-Ray in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Dental-X-Ray.jpg "Dental X-Ray")

## Digital X-Rays in Reno, NV

![Dental X-Ray in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-X-Ray.jpg "Dental X-Ray")

![Digital X-Rays in Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/xray-machine.jpg "Digital X-Rays")

##### What are Digital X-Rays?

Digital X-rays are a state-of-the-art imaging technology that replaces traditional film X-Rays with digital sensors. This revolutionary technique allows us to capture highly detailed images of your teeth and supporting structures swiftly and with minimal radiation exposure. The images produced are instantly available for analysis, providing our dental team with immediate insights into your oral health.

##### Why Choose Digital X-Rays?

**Reduced Radiation Exposure  
**Digital X-Rays significantly reduce radiation exposure compared to conventional film X-Rays, making them safer for our patients. Your well-being is our top priority, and we are committed to utilizing technology that minimizes potential risks.

**Quick and Convenient  
**The digital nature of these X-Rays means no waiting for film development. Images are available in seconds, allowing us to make prompt and accurate diagnoses. This efficiency streamlines your dental appointments and ensures a smoother overall experience.

**Enhanced Diagnostic Capabilities  
**Digital X-Rays provide highly detailed images that can be magnified, manipulated, and enhanced for better diagnostic accuracy. This allows our experienced dental professionals to identify potential issues early on, leading to more effective treatment plans.

**Environmentally Friendly  
**Unlike traditional X-Ray processes that involve the use of hazardous chemicals, Digital X-Rays are environmentally friendly. This aligns with our commitment to sustainable and responsible dental practices.

##### Schedule Your Appointment Today

Try our Digital X-Rays service to ensure a proactive approach to your oral health. By choosing us, you benefit from the latest technology, a team of skilled professionals, and a commitment to your well-being.

Your smile is our priority, and with Digital X-Rays at Well Beyond Dental, we are dedicated to providing you with the best in modern dental care. Invest in your oral health today for a brighter, healthier tomorrow.

# Teeth Cleaning

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we prioritize your oral health and strive to provide the highest quality dental care. Our comprehensive range of services includes professional dental cleanings, an integral part of maintaining a healthy smile.  A dental cleaning, also known as a prophylaxis, is a professional procedure performed by our skilled dental hygienists. It involves the meticulous removal of plaque, tartar, and stains from your teeth, along with thorough polishing to achieve a fresh and radiant smile.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Clean Teeth in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-General-Dentistry.jpg "Clean Teeth")

## Expert Teeth Cleaning in Reno, NV

![Teeth Cleaning Services in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Toothbrush.jpg "Teeth Cleaning Services")

![Expert Teeth Cleaning in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Teeth-Cleaning-Chair.jpg "Expert Teeth Cleaning")

##### Why are Dental Cleanings Important?

**Preventing Gum Disease**: Dental cleanings play a critical role in preventing gum disease, also known as periodontal disease. Regular cleanings remove plaque and tartar buildup, which harbor harmful bacteria that can infect the gums. By eliminating these substances, we help reduce the risk of gum inflammation, bleeding, and the development of gum disease.

**Maintaining Optimal Oral Health**: Even with diligent at-home oral hygiene practices, certain areas of your mouth can be challenging to clean effectively. Dental cleanings reach those difficult-to-access areas, ensuring that plaque and tartar do not accumulate and lead to decay or gum problems. Regular cleanings contribute to maintaining your teeth’s health, longevity, and functionality.

**Preventing Tooth Decay**: Plaque buildup is the leading cause of tooth decay. When left untreated, plaque can erode tooth enamel, leading to cavities and other dental issues. Dental cleanings eliminate plaque and tartar, minimizing the risk of decay and preserving the integrity of your teeth.

**Brightening Your Smile**: Over time, stains from various foods, beverages, and lifestyle habits (such as smoking) can dull your smile’s brightness. Dental cleanings involve polishing your teeth to remove these stains, resulting in a noticeably brighter and more vibrant smile.

**Freshening Your Breath**: Persistent bad breath, or halitosis, can be an embarrassing issue. Often, it is caused by bacteria in the mouth. Professional dental cleanings target and remove the bacteria that contribute to bad breath, leaving your mouth feeling fresh and revitalized.

**Early Detection of Dental Issues**: Regular dental cleanings offer an opportunity for our skilled professionals to assess your oral health thoroughly. During cleanings, our team can identify any early signs of dental problems such as cavities, gum disease, or oral cancer. Early detection allows for prompt intervention, increasing the chances of successful treatment and preserving your oral health.

##### How Often Should You Get a Dental Cleaning?

The frequency of dental cleanings depends on individual factors, including your oral health condition, susceptibility to gum disease, and personal hygiene habits. In general, we recommend scheduling dental cleanings every six months to maintain optimal oral health. However, some individuals may require more frequent cleanings if they have specific conditions or risk factors.

##### Experience the Benefits of Professional Dental Cleanings

We are committed to delivering exceptional dental care to our patients. Our team of experienced professionals understands the importance of regular dental cleanings in maintaining a healthy smile and preventing oral health issues. We provide a comfortable and friendly environment where you can feel at ease throughout your cleaning procedure.

Contact us today to schedule your next dental cleaning appointment and take a proactive step towards a healthier, brighter smile. Remember, your oral health is our priority, and we are here to support you on your journey to optimal dental well-being.

# Cosmetic Dentistry

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand that your smile is one of your most important features. We also know that a healthy smile can be an important asset in both personal and professional life. That’s why we offer a range of cosmetic dental services designed to help you achieve the beautiful, confident smile you deserve. 

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Cosmetic Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Glowing-Smile.jpg "Cosmetic Dentistry")

## Cosmetic Dental Services in Reno, NV

![Cosmetic Dentistry in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/Dental-Model.jpg "Cosmetic Dentistry in Reno")

![Cosmetic Dentist in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/gallery_home019-1.jpeg "Cosmetic Dentist in Reno")

##### Implant Restoration

Sometimes when teeth are missing or are unable to support a tooth prosthesis such as a crown or denture, then an implant restoration may be used. The implant restorations done by a Reno dentist at Well Beyond Dental are stable, long-lasting, and can help significantly enhance your quality of life.

Dental implants are an outstanding restorative option for patients who have missing teeth or require tooth extraction because of advanced disease or decay. A dental implant is a threaded titanium post that is surgically inserted into the jawbone to replace lost tooth roots

##### Veneers

Veneers are thin layers of material that adhere to the surface of your teeth to give them a defined classic tooth shape, and a white polished look. When you are ready to have the smile you have always wanted, we can help! Veneers are long term solutions to your smile that can make you feel comfortable and love your smile.

Porcelain and composite bonding are the most common veneer materials. Composite veneers are made of a resin that is shaped and bonded to a tooth to enhance the appearance. Porcelain veneers are usually made in a lab and are more resistant to staining, compared to other materials.

##### Teeth Whitening

Teeth whitening can help give you the confidence in your smile that you deserve. It’s convenient, easy, and quick. Our Reno dentistry office offers pre-loaded whitening trays that will help protect your pearly whites so you can smile all day long!

In-office bleaching is called chairside bleaching and usually requires only one office visit. The dentist will apply either a protective gel to your gums or a rubber shield to protect your gums. Bleach is then applied to the teeth.

Your dentist can provide you with a custom-made tray for at-home whitening. In this case, the dentist will give you instructions on how to place the bleaching solution in the tray and for what length of time.

##### Dental Bonding

Dental bonding is a cosmetic dental procedure where a tooth-colored resin material is applied and hardened with a special light, effectively bonding the material to the tooth to improve or restore a person’s smile. This versatile treatment is used to repair decayed, chipped, cracked, or discolored teeth, close gaps between teeth, and even change the shape of teeth. The process is relatively simple and can usually be completed in one visit to the dentist. The resin is carefully sculpted to match the surrounding teeth in color and shape, providing a natural and aesthetically pleasing result.

Dental bonding is a cost-effective and minimally invasive option for enhancing the appearance of your teeth, with results that can last several years with proper care.

# Dental Bonding

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand the importance of a confident smile. Our dental bonding service is designed to help you achieve the smile you desire with ease and comfort. Dental bonding is a versatile and effective cosmetic dental procedure that can address various dental imperfections, giving you a natural-looking, beautiful smile.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Dental Bonding in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/cosmetic-bonding.jpg "Dental Bonding")

## Professional Dental Bonding in Reno, NV

![Reno Dental Bonding](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-X-Ray.jpg "Dental Bonding Services")

![Dental Bonding in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Appointment.jpg "Expert Dental Bonding")

##### What is Dental Bonding?

Dental bonding, also known as composite bonding, is a cosmetic dental procedure that involves the application of a tooth-colored composite resin material to repair or improve the appearance of teeth. It is a non-invasive and affordable solution for correcting a range of dental issues, including:

-   **Chipped or cracked teeth**: Dental bonding can restore the shape and functionality of damaged teeth.
-   **Gaps between teeth**: Bonding can fill in gaps between teeth, improving the overall appearance of your smile.
-   **Discolored teeth**: Stained or discolored teeth can be transformed with dental bonding, giving you a brighter, more radiant smile.
-   **Misshapen teeth**: Bonding can reshape irregular or misshapen teeth, creating a more harmonious smile.

##### How Can Dental Bonding Help You?

-   **Improved Aesthetics**: Dental bonding can enhance the appearance of your smile by addressing various cosmetic imperfections.
-   **Conservative Treatment**: Unlike some other cosmetic dental procedures, dental bonding typically requires minimal tooth preparation, preserving more of your natural tooth structure.
-   **Quick and Painless**: Dental bonding is a relatively quick and painless procedure that can often be completed in a single visit to our office.
-   **Long-lasting Results**: With proper care and maintenance, dental bonding can provide durable and long-lasting results, allowing you to enjoy your beautiful smile for years to come.

##### Why Choose Well Beyond Dental?

At Well Beyond Dental, we are committed to providing exceptional dental care in a comfortable and welcoming environment. When you choose us for your dental bonding needs, you can expect:

-   **Experienced Professionals**: Our team of experienced dentists has the expertise and skill to deliver outstanding results.
-   **Personalized Treatment Plans**: We understand that every patient is unique, which is why we tailor our treatment plans to meet your specific needs and goals.
-   **State-of-the-Art Facilities**: We utilize the latest dental technology and techniques to ensure that you receive the highest standard of care.
-   **Compassionate Care**: Your comfort and satisfaction are our top priorities. We strive to create a positive and stress-free experience for all of our patients.

**Schedule Your Appointment Today!**

Ready to transform your smile with dental bonding? Schedule an appointment with us today to learn more about our dental bonding service and discover how we can help you achieve the smile of your dreams. We look forward to welcoming you to Well Beyond Dental!

# Implant Restoration

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand the impact that missing teeth can have on your oral health, appearance, and overall quality of life. That’s why we offer top-quality implant restoration services to help you regain a confident smile and restore the functionality of your teeth. Our team of experienced professionals is dedicated to providing personalized care and exceptional results, ensuring that your dental implant journey is a success.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Implant Restoration](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Glowing-Smile.jpg "Implant Restoration")

## Expertly Restoring Implants in Reno, NV

![WBD Implant](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/06/pexels-cottonbro-studio-6502305.jpg "Implant")

![Implant Restoration](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Implants.jpg "WBD Implant Restoration")

##### What is Implant Restoration?

Implant restoration is a specialized dental procedure that involves replacing one or more missing teeth with dental implants. Dental implants are titanium posts that are surgically placed into your jawbone, acting as artificial tooth roots. These implants serve as a sturdy foundation for the attachment of lifelike replacement teeth, such as crowns, bridges, or dentures. Implant restoration is a long-lasting and effective solution for tooth loss, offering numerous benefits over traditional tooth replacement options.

##### Why Is Implant Restoration Important?

**Improved Oral Health:** Dental implants not only replace missing teeth but also stimulate the jawbone, preventing bone loss and preserving the natural facial structure. Unlike other tooth replacement options, such as removable dentures or dental bridges, implants do not rely on adjacent teeth for support, ensuring that your remaining natural teeth are left untouched.

**Enhanced Functionality:** With implant restoration, you can regain the ability to bite, chew, and speak with confidence. The secure and stable foundation provided by dental implants allows for comfortable eating and speaking, eliminating any concerns about slipping or discomfort commonly associated with removable dentures.

**Aesthetically Pleasing Results:** Our implant restoration services offer a natural-looking solution to tooth loss. The replacement teeth are custom-designed to match the color, shape, and size of your existing teeth, ensuring a seamless and harmonious smile. Implants also prevent the loss of facial volume, reducing the appearance of premature aging caused by missing teeth.

**Long-Lasting Solution:** Dental implants are designed to be durable and long-lasting. With proper care and regular dental visits, implants can last a lifetime, providing you with a permanent solution to tooth loss. This eliminates the need for frequent replacements or adjustments often required with other tooth replacement options, saving you time and money in the long run.

**Improved Self-Confidence:** Tooth loss can greatly impact your self-esteem and confidence. Implant restoration can help you regain a natural-looking smile, allowing you to feel more comfortable and confident in social and professional settings. Restoring your smile can positively affect your overall well-being and quality of life.

##### Schedule Your Appointment Today

At Well Beyond Dental, we prioritize your oral health and satisfaction above all else. When you choose our implant restoration services, you can expect expertise and experience, personalized treatment plans, state-of-the-art facilities, and compassionate care. 

We prioritize your comfort and well-being during every step of the implant restoration process. Our friendly team is committed to creating a relaxing and positive environment, making your dental visits a stress-free experience.

Don’t let missing teeth hinder your oral health and confidence any longer. Contact Well Beyond Dental today to schedule a consultation and explore our implant restoration services. Our dedicated team is here to guide you through the process and help you achieve a beautiful, functional smile that goes well beyond your expectations.

# Veneers

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand the importance of a confident and beautiful smile. That’s why we offer high-quality veneer services that can transform your teeth and enhance your overall appearance. Whether you have stained, misshapen, or chipped teeth, veneers provide a simple and effective solution to give you the smile you’ve always desired.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![WBD Veneers](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dentist-Appointment.jpg "WBD Veneers")

## Expert Veneers in Reno, NV

![WBD Veneer Color](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Teeth-Color.jpg "WBD Veneer Color")

![Dentist Chair](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Teeth-Cleaning-Chair.jpg "Dentist Chair")

##### What are Veneers?

Veneers are thin, custom-made shells that are designed to cover the front surface of your teeth. They are crafted from durable and natural-looking materials, such as porcelain or composite resin, and are customized to match the shape, size, and color of your existing teeth.

Veneers are a versatile cosmetic dental solution that can address a range of aesthetic concerns, including:

-   **Stained or Discolored Teeth**: Veneers can effectively cover deep stains that are resistant to teeth whitening treatments, helping you achieve a brighter, more radiant smile.
-   **Misaligned or Crooked Teeth**: If you have minor misalignments or gaps between your teeth, veneers can provide a quick and non-invasive alternative to orthodontic treatment. By placing veneers on the affected teeth, we can create the illusion of a straight and even smile.
-   **Chipped or Damaged Teeth**: Veneers can restore the appearance of teeth that are chipped, cracked, or worn down. By concealing imperfections, veneers can rejuvenate your smile and improve your overall dental aesthetics.

##### Why choose Veneers?

Veneers offer numerous advantages that make them a popular choice among our patients. Here are some key reasons why veneers are important for achieving the smile of your dreams:

-   **Enhanced Aesthetics**: Veneers are meticulously crafted to enhance the natural beauty of your teeth. They can dramatically improve the color, shape, and symmetry of your smile, giving you a boost of confidence and self-esteem.
-   **Minimally Invasive**: Unlike other dental procedures, veneers require minimal alteration of your natural teeth. Our experienced dentists will remove a small amount of enamel to ensure a proper fit, but the process is conservative and preserves the overall structure of your teeth.
-   **Long-lasting Results**: With proper care and maintenance, veneers can provide long-lasting results. Porcelain veneers are highly resistant to stains and can withstand normal wear and tear, making them a durable and reliable solution for enhancing your smile.
-   **Customized and Natural-looking**: Our veneers are meticulously designed to blend seamlessly with your natural teeth. We take into account factors such as tooth color, shape, and size, ensuring that your veneers look and feel like a natural part of your smile.
-   **Improved Oral Health**: Veneers not only enhance your smile but also provide additional protection to your teeth. The thin layer of porcelain or composite resin acts as a barrier, safeguarding your teeth against further damage or decay.

##### Schedule Your Appointment Today

Visit Well Beyond Dental for a Stunning Smile!

At Well Beyond Dental, we believe that everyone deserves a smile they can be proud of. Our experienced team of dental professionals is dedicated to providing top-notch veneer services that can transform your teeth and boost your confidence. With our expertise and attention to detail, you can trust us to deliver outstanding results and ensure your complete satisfaction.

Contact us today to schedule a consultation and take the first step towards achieving a radiant smile with veneers.

# Teeth Whitening

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Revitalize your smile with teeth whitening at Well Beyond Dental. Experience professional-grade treatments to achieve a brighter, more radiant smile. Explore our safe and effective teeth whitening options for a confident and stunning appearance.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![WBD Teeth Whitening](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Teeth-Whitening.jpg "WBD Teeth Whitening")

## About Teeth Whitening

![Teeth Whitening](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Teeth-Color.jpg "Teeth Whitening")

![Glowing Smile](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Glowing-Smile.jpg "Glowing Smile")

##### Why is Teeth Whitening Important?

Teeth whitening is a cosmetic dental procedure designed to lighten and brighten the color of your teeth. Over time, teeth can become discolored or stained due to various factors, including:

**Diet**: Consuming dark-colored foods and beverages like coffee, tea, red wine, and berries can lead to tooth staining.

**Tobacco Use**: Smoking or using tobacco products can cause yellowing and discoloration of teeth.

**Aging**: As we age, the outer layer of enamel on our teeth naturally wears down, revealing the yellowish dentin underneath.

**Poor Oral Hygiene**: Inadequate dental care can lead to the buildup of plaque and tartar, which can stain teeth.

**Medications**: Certain medications, such as tetracycline antibiotics, can cause tooth discoloration, especially when taken during tooth development in childhood.

##### Why choose Well Beyond Dental?

A bright, white smile can make all the difference in how you feel about yourself and the impression you make on others. Well Beyond Dental offers cutting-edge teeth whitening services that can give you the brilliant smile you’ve always wanted. Our experienced team of dental professionals uses the latest technology and techniques to safely and effectively brighten your teeth, giving you immediate and long-lasting results.

We understand that a healthy, beautiful smile is an important part of your overall health and well-being, and we are committed to providing the highest quality care to our patients. Choose Well Beyond Dental for teeth whitening and experience the confidence and satisfaction that comes with a radiant smile.

# Preventative Dentistry

At Well Beyond Dental, we believe in the power of prevention when it comes to maintaining your oral health. Preventative dentistry focuses on the practices, treatments, and education necessary to prevent dental issues before they arise. Our goal is to help you achieve and maintain a healthy smile through regular care and early intervention. Preventative dentistry encompasses all the steps taken to care for your teeth and gums, with the aim of preventing cavities, gum disease, enamel wear, and more.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Well Beyond Preventative Dentistry](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Work.jpg "Well Beyond Preventative Dentistry")

## Preventative Dental Services in Reno, NV

![Well Beyond Dental Check-up](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/Same-Day-Smile-Treatment-Plan.jpg "Well Beyond Dental Check-up")

![Well Beyond Dental Exam](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Exam.jpg "Well Beyond Dental Exam")

##### Dental Check-ups

Regular dental check-ups, typically recommended every six months, are crucial for monitoring your oral health. During these visits, our dental professionals thoroughly examine your teeth, gums, and mouth for any signs of problems, such as cavities, gum disease, or oral cancer.

##### Regular Dental Cleaning

Professional dental cleanings remove plaque and tartar that regular brushing and flossing can’t reach. Our hygienists use specialized tools to clean your teeth and gums, helping to prevent tooth decay and gum disease.

##### Dental Sealants

**What They Are:** Dental sealants are thin, protective coatings applied to the chewing surfaces of the back teeth (molars and premolars). These surfaces have grooves that can trap food particles and bacteria, leading to cavities.

**How They Help:** Sealants act as a barrier, protecting the enamel from plaque and acids. They are especially beneficial for children and teenagers, as their newly erupted permanent teeth are more susceptible to decay.

##### Fluoride Treatments

**What They Are:** Fluoride treatments involve the application of fluoride to the teeth in the form of a gel, foam, or varnish. Fluoride is a natural mineral that strengthens tooth enamel and helps prevent cavities.

**How They Help:** These treatments can be performed during regular dental visits. They are particularly useful for children, individuals with a history of cavities, and those with high risk factors for tooth decay.

##### Oral Cancer Screenings

**What They Are:** Oral cancer screenings are a critical part of our preventative care regimen. During these screenings, our dental professionals carefully examine your mouth, throat, and neck for signs of oral cancer.

**How They Help:** Early detection of oral cancer significantly increases the chances of successful treatment. Our team uses the latest technology to identify any abnormalities or changes in your oral tissues, ensuring prompt and effective intervention if needed.

##### Mouthguards and Nightguards

**What They Are:** Custom-made mouthguards and nightguards protect your teeth from damage due to sports injuries or bruxism (teeth grinding).

**How They Help:** Mouthguards cushion your teeth during physical activities, reducing the risk of broken teeth and injuries to the lips, tongue, face, or jaw. Nightguards prevent the wear and tear on your teeth caused by grinding, preserving your smile and preventing jaw pain.

##### Nutritional Counseling

**What It Is:** Our team offers advice on nutrition and its impact on oral health. Certain foods and beverages can contribute to tooth decay and gum disease.

**How It Helps:** By making informed dietary choices, you can improve your oral health and overall well-being. We provide tips on reducing sugar intake, selecting tooth-friendly snacks, and staying hydrated.

# Dental Sealants

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we are dedicated to protecting and enhancing your smile through comprehensive and preventative dental care. One of the most effective ways to safeguard your teeth against decay is through the application of dental sealants. Whether you are looking to protect your child’s newly emerged molars or seeking additional cavity protection for yourself, dental sealants offer a simple yet powerful solution.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Dental Sealants in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Female-with-Great-Smile.jpg "Dental Sealants in Reno")

## Dental Sealants in Reno, NV

![Reno Dental Sealants](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/child-in-dentist-chair.jpg "Dental Sealants")

![Dental Sealants Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/pexels-andrea-piacquadio-3952006.jpg "Dental Sealants")

##### What are Dental Sealants?

Dental sealants are a protective coating applied to the chewing surfaces of the back teeth, primarily the molars and premolars. These teeth have deep grooves and pits where food particles and bacteria can get trapped, making them more susceptible to decay. Sealants create a smooth, easy-to-clean surface that acts as a barrier against plaque and food, helping to prevent cavities.

##### How Can Dental Sealants Help?

**When Are Dental Sealants Used?**

Dental sealants are typically applied to children’s teeth as soon as their permanent molars and premolars come in, usually between the ages of 6 and 14. However, adults without decay or fillings in their molars can also benefit from sealants. They are particularly useful for individuals at a higher risk of tooth decay, including those with deep grooves in their teeth or a history of cavities.

**How do they help?**

-   **Prevent Cavities**: By filling in the grooves and pits of the molars and premolars, sealants prevent food and bacteria from settling in these areas, significantly reducing the risk of cavities.
-   **Save Time and Money**: Preventing cavities with sealants can help avoid more extensive and costly dental procedures in the future, such as fillings, crowns, or root canals.
-   **Quick and Painless Application**: The process of applying dental sealants is fast and painless, typically taking only a few minutes per tooth. There is no need for drilling or removing any tooth structure.
-   **Long-Lasting Protection**: Sealants can last several years before needing to be reapplied, providing long-term protection against decay with proper dental care and regular check-ups.

##### Why Choose Well Beyond Dental?

We are dedicated to providing comprehensive dental care tailored to your specific needs. Our skilled and experienced dental professionals are committed to delivering the highest standard of care. We use the latest techniques and materials to ensure your sealants are applied effectively and comfortably.  We provide a welcoming and relaxing environment for patients of all ages. Our friendly staff is here to ensure your visit is comfortable and stress-free.

 At Well Beyond Dental, we emphasize preventative care to maintain your oral health and prevent dental issues before they arise. Dental sealants are just one of the many preventative services we offer to keep your smile healthy.  Schedule an appointment with Well Beyond Dental today to learn more about dental sealants and how they can benefit you and your family. Let us help you achieve and maintain a bright, healthy smile for years to come.

# Fluoride Treatment

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we are committed to providing our patients with the best preventive care to ensure long-lasting oral health. One of the most effective treatments we offer to protect your teeth from decay and strengthen your enamel is fluoride treatment.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Flouride Treatment in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/dentist-with-smile-scaled.jpg "Flouride Treatment in Reno")

## Effective Fluoride Treatments in Reno, NV

![WBD Fluoride Treatments](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/Same-Day-Smile-Application.jpg "WBD Fluoride Treatments")

![Child Fluoride Treatments](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/child-in-dentist-chair.jpg "Child Fluoride Treatments")

##### What are Fluoride Treatments?

#### What is Fluoride Treatment?

Fluoride treatment is a professional dental procedure that involves the application of fluoride to the teeth in the form of a gel, foam, varnish, or rinse. Fluoride, a naturally occurring mineral, helps to strengthen tooth enamel and prevent cavities by making teeth more resistant to acid attacks from plaque bacteria and sugars in the mouth.

#### When is Fluoride Treatment Needed?

Fluoride treatments are especially beneficial for:

-   **Children and Teens:** As their teeth are still developing, they are more susceptible to cavities.
-   **Adults with High Cavity Risk:** Those with a history of frequent cavities, poor oral hygiene, or inadequate fluoride exposure.
-   **Patients with Dry Mouth Conditions:** Reduced saliva flow, which can be caused by medications or medical conditions, increases the risk of tooth decay.
-   **Individuals with Dental Appliances:** Braces, bridges, or crowns can trap food and bacteria, leading to a higher risk of cavities.
-   **People with Gum Disease:** Gum disease can expose more of the tooth and roots to bacteria, increasing the risk of decay.

##### When Can Fluoride Treatments Help?

Fluoride treatments offer numerous benefits:

-   **Strengthens Tooth Enamel:** Fluoride helps rebuild weakened tooth enamel and reverses early signs of tooth decay.
-   **Reduces Cavity Risk:** Regular fluoride treatments can significantly lower the risk of cavities, particularly in children and those with a high risk of dental decay.
-   **Protects Against Acid Attacks:** Fluoride forms a barrier on your teeth, protecting them from acid produced by plaque bacteria and sugars.
-   **Supports Overall Oral Health:** By preventing cavities, fluoride treatments contribute to maintaining healthier teeth and gums, leading to better overall oral health.

##### Why Choose Us for Your Fluoride Treatments?

We are dedicated to providing top-notch dental care tailored to your unique needs.  Our skilled and compassionate dental team has extensive experience in preventive dental care, including fluoride treatments.

We assess your individual risk factors and dental history to create a customized fluoride treatment plan that best suits your needs.  Our modern, well-equipped dental office ensures you receive the highest quality care in a comfortable and welcoming environment.  Don’t wait for cavities to impact your oral health. Schedule an appointment with Well Beyond Dental today.

# Mouth Guards/Night Guards

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we prioritize the protection of your teeth and overall oral health. One effective solution for safeguarding your smile from damage is the use of custom mouth guards or night guards. Whether you grind your teeth at night or participate in sports, these guards act as a barrier to prevent wear, injury, and long-term damage to your teeth. Designed for comfort and durability, our custom-fit mouth guards provide an added layer of protection, helping you maintain a healthy, strong smile.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Mouth and Night Guards](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Mouth-and-Night-Guards.jpg "Mouth and Night Guards")

## Protect Your Smile with Custom Mouth Guards and Night Guards

![Mouth Guards and Night Guards](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/3-e1715610410153.jpg "Mouth Guards and Night Guards")

![Mouth Guards/Night Guards](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/pexels-andrea-piacquadio-3952006.jpg "Mouth Guards/Night Guards")

##### Custom Fit Protection

At Well Beyond Dental, our custom mouth guards and night guards are tailored to fit comfortably and securely, offering effective protection against teeth grinding, clenching, and sports-related injuries. These guards are designed to preserve your oral health and prevent damage to your teeth and jaw.

##### Prevent Long-Term Damage

Night guards help alleviate the harmful effects of teeth grinding (bruxism), which can lead to wear, fractures, and other dental issues. By using a night guard, you protect your smile and avoid costly restorative treatments.

##### Comfort and Durability

Our mouth guards and night guards are made with high-quality materials, ensuring durability and comfort. Whether you need protection during sports or while you sleep, we provide guards that are long-lasting and easy to wear, helping you maintain a healthy smile with ease.

##### Why You Should Choose Well Beyond Dental

Choosing Well Beyond Dental means you’re trusting a team dedicated to providing personalized, high-quality dental care. Our commitment to comfort, advanced technology, and patient-centered service ensures that every treatment is tailored to your unique needs.

Whether you need preventive solutions like mouth guards or comprehensive restorative care, our experienced staff focuses on delivering long-term results to keep your smile healthy and strong. At Well Beyond Dental, we prioritize your oral health with a compassionate approach, ensuring you receive the best care possible.

# Nutritional Counseling

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Our personalized Nutritional Counseling services at Well Beyond Dental are designed to improve your overall and oral health through tailored nutrition plans. Let us help you make healthier choices that positively impact your well-being.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Expert Nutrition Counseling](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Same-Day-Smiles.jpg "Expert Nutrition Counseling")

## Enhance Your Wellness with Personalized Nutritional Counseling in Reno, NV

![Nutrition Counseling Dentist](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Nutrition-Counseling-Dentist.jpg "Nutrition Counseling Dentist")

![Well Beyond Dental Nutrition](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Well-Beyond-Dental-Nutrition.jpg "Well Beyond Dental Nutrition")

##### Personalized Nutrition Plans

At Well Beyond Dental, we believe that good nutrition is key to maintaining optimal oral and overall health. Our nutritional counseling services offer personalized plans tailored to your specific needs, lifestyle, and goals. Whether you’re seeking to manage a condition, lose weight, or enhance your well-being, we provide the guidance and support you need to make sustainable changes. Our goal is to empower you with the tools and knowledge to make healthier choices that positively impact your oral and general health.

##### Improve Oral Health Through Nutrition

Nutrition plays a crucial role in maintaining the health of your teeth and gums. Our nutritional counseling services focus on educating you about the best foods and habits to support your oral health. From reducing sugar intake to understanding the role of vitamins and minerals, we guide you in adopting a diet that strengthens your teeth and prevents dental issues. This holistic approach ensures that you not only maintain a healthy smile but also improve your overall wellness.

##### Support For Chronic Conditions

If you are managing a chronic condition like diabetes, heart disease, or digestive issues, our nutritional counseling can help. We provide expert advice on how to adjust your diet to manage symptoms and improve your quality of life. By addressing your unique health challenges, we help you create a plan that supports both your oral and systemic health. This personalized approach is designed to help you feel your best and stay on track toward better health.

##### Why Choose Well Beyond Dental For Nutritional Counseling?

Choosing Well Beyond Dental for nutritional counseling means receiving care from a team that truly understands the connection between nutrition and oral health. Our experienced professionals work closely with you to develop a plan that is not only effective but also sustainable. We take the time to listen to your concerns and goals, ensuring that the advice we provide fits your lifestyle. With our holistic approach, we aim to improve your health from the inside out, empowering you to live a healthier, more vibrant life.

# Oral Cancer Screenings in Reno, NV

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Your health is our top priority at Well Beyond Dental, and that includes safeguarding you from serious conditions like oral cancer. Early detection through regular screenings can be a lifesaver, allowing for prompt and effective treatment.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Oral Cancer Screenings in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Happy-senior-patient-in-chair.jpg "Oral Cancer Screenings in Reno")

## Early Detection for Lifelong Health and Peace of Mind

![WBD Oral Cancer Screening](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/doctor-02.jpg "WBD Oral Cancer Screenings")

![Screening for Oral Cancer](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/digital-image-review.jpg "Screening for Oral Cancer")

##### What are Oral Cancer Screenings?

Oral cancer screenings are thorough examinations conducted by dental professionals to detect signs of cancer or precancerous conditions in your mouth. These screenings involve a visual inspection of your oral cavity and palpation of the tissues to identify any abnormal areas or growths. Our goal is to catch any signs of cancer early, when treatment is most effective.

Oral cancer screenings are typically performed during routine dental check-ups. They are especially important for individuals over the age of 40, those who use tobacco products, consume alcohol regularly, or have a history of oral cancer. Even if you do not fall into these categories, regular screenings are a proactive step in maintaining your overall health.

##### Why Have an Oral Cancer Screening?

Early detection is crucial in the fight against oral cancer. By identifying potential issues before they develop into more serious conditions, oral cancer screenings can significantly increase the chances of successful treatment. These screenings help in:

-   -   Detecting early signs of cancer or precancerous conditions.
    -   Providing peace of mind knowing that your oral health is being monitored closely.
    -   Creating a baseline for future comparisons, allowing for more accurate monitoring over time.
    -   Prompting lifestyle changes to reduce risk factors for oral cancer.

##### Why Choose Us?

We are committed to providing comprehensive care that prioritizes your overall well-being. Our dental team is highly trained and experienced in conducting thorough and effective oral cancer screenings. We use the latest technology to ensure accurate and efficient screenings, making the process comfortable and effective for you.

Don’t wait for symptoms to appear. Early detection can save lives. Schedule your oral cancer screening with Well Beyond Dental today and take a proactive step towards ensuring your long-term health and well-being.

# Restorative Dentistry in Reno, NV

Well Beyond Dental is committed to providing our patients with the highest quality of care.  We use the latest technologies and techniques to ensure that you receive the best possible restorative treatment for your situation.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Restorative Dentist in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/Same-Day-Smile-Application.jpg "Restorative Dentist in Reno")

## Professional Restorative Dental Services in Reno, NV

![Restorative Dentistry in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Well-Beyond_Still_3.jpg "Restorative Dentistry in Reno")

![Restorative Dental Care in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/gallery_home019-1.jpeg "Restorative Dental Care in Reno")

##### Dental Fillings

Dental fillings, also called dental restoration, helps bring back the functionality of your tooth. We use only composite (white, tooth-colored fillings) for all dental fillings – no metal amalgams here!

##### Crowns & Bridges

Dental crowns are covers, or caps, that go over a single tooth. They are used to restore the shape, size and function of a tooth that has too much decay to be fixed with a dental filling. Dental bridges are meant to fill in a gap between teeth caused by a missing tooth. Here, two or more dental crowns are used to fill in the gap, which is held in place by adjacent teeth.

Unlike removable devices such as dentures, crowns and bridges are placed over your real teeth and are not removed.

##### Dentures & Partials

Dentures are removable replacements for missing teeth. Complete dentures have a complete set of false teeth attached, whereas partials are used when some natural teeth remain.

Our Reno dentistry offers full upper, lower, and partial dentures that will help beautify your smile and give you back your confidence!

##### Tooth Extractions

Tooth extractions are a common dental procedure performed to remove a tooth from its socket in the bone. Extractions may be necessary for various reasons, including severe tooth decay, infection, crowding, or trauma. Wisdom teeth, or third molars, are often extracted because they can cause pain, infection, or alignment issues. The procedure is typically straightforward and can be performed under local anesthesia, sedation, or general anesthesia, depending on the complexity of the extraction and the patient’s comfort level. After the extraction, patients are given specific aftercare instructions to promote healing and minimize discomfort.

While the idea of tooth extraction can be daunting, modern dental techniques and anesthesia options ensure that the procedure is as painless and stress-free as possible.

# Crowns & Bridges

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Enhance your smile with crowns and bridges at Well Beyond Dental. Our experienced dentists provide top-quality dental restorations, including crowns and bridges, to repair damaged teeth and replace missing ones.  Regain your smile’s function and aesthetics.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Crowns & Bridges](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/Dental-Model.jpg "Dental Model")

## Professional Crowns & Bridges in Reno, NV

![Dental Crown](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/Dental-Crown.jpg "Dental Crown")

![Dentist Chair](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Teeth-Cleaning-Chair.jpg "Dentist Chair")

##### What are Dental Crowns?

Dental crowns, also known as dental caps, are dental restorations that completely encase a damaged or weakened tooth, covering it above the gum line. These custom-made prosthetics are designed to restore the tooth’s shape, size, strength, and appearance, while also providing protection and improving its function.

Dental crowns are used in various situations, including:

-   **Protecting Weakened Teeth**: If a tooth is severely decayed, fractured, or weakened due to root canal therapy, a dental crown can provide the necessary strength and support to prevent further damage and potential tooth loss.
-   **Restoring Broken or Chipped Teeth**: Teeth that have suffered significant fractures or chips can be restored to their original shape and appearance with the help of dental crowns.
-   **Covering Large Fillings**: When a tooth has a large filling that compromises its structural integrity, a dental crown can be placed over the filling to protect and strengthen the tooth.
-   **Covering Discolored or Misshapen Teeth**: Dental crowns can be used to cover teeth with severe discoloration, irregular shapes, or cosmetic imperfections, improving the overall aesthetics of the smile.
-   **Supporting Dental Bridges**: Dental crowns are essential components of dental bridges, anchoring the artificial tooth (pontic) in place by fitting over the adjacent natural teeth.

##### What are Dental Bridges?

Dental bridges are fixed dental prosthetics used to replace one or more missing teeth by “bridging” the gap between two healthy teeth or dental implants. These restorations consist of artificial teeth, known as pontics, which are fused between two dental crowns or dental implants on either side of the gap. The healthy teeth or implants on either end of the bridge serve as anchors, supporting and holding the bridge securely in place.

Dental bridges are an effective solution for restoring the function and aesthetics of the smile, and they offer several benefits, including:

-   **Restoring Missing Teeth**: Dental bridges fill the gaps left by missing teeth, restoring the ability to chew, speak, and smile comfortably.
-   **Maintaining Facial Structure**: The presence of missing teeth can lead to changes in facial structure, causing a sunken appearance. Dental bridges help maintain proper facial contours and support the lips and cheeks.
-   **Preventing Teeth Shifting**: When there is a gap from a missing tooth, neighboring teeth can shift out of position, affecting the bite and alignment. Dental bridges help prevent this unwanted movement.
-   **Distributing Bite Forces**: Dental bridges distribute the forces of chewing evenly among the supporting teeth, reducing the strain on individual teeth.

##### How Can We Help?

At Well Beyond Dental, our approach to treating patients in need of crowns and bridges is centered on providing personalized and comprehensive care. Throughout the entire process, we prioritize patient comfort and satisfaction. Our team is dedicated to delivering high-quality dental restorations that not only improve oral function but also enhance the aesthetics of our patients’ smiles. At Well Beyond Dental, we are committed to providing top-notch care and creating lasting, beautiful smiles for our patients.

# Dental Fillings

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand that your oral health plays a vital role in your overall well-being. Dental fillings are essential for preserving the natural tooth structure and preventing the need for more extensive dental treatments, such as dental crowns or root canals, which may be necessary if the decay or damage progresses.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![WBD Dental Fillings](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Appointment.jpg "WBD Dental Fillings")

## Pain-free Dental Fillings in Reno, NV

![Dental Fillings in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Well-Beyond-Emergency.jpg "Dental Fillings in Reno")

![Dental Filling ](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Exam.jpg "Dental Filling")

##### What is a Dental Filling?

A dental filling is a common restorative dental procedure used to repair and restore a tooth that has been damaged by decay or minor fractures. The process involves removing the decayed or damaged portion of the tooth and filling the resulting cavity with a suitable material. Various materials can be used for dental fillings, and the choice depends on factors such as the size and location of the cavity, the patient’s preferences, and the dentist’s recommendation.

##### Why Are Dental Fillings Important?

Dental fillings are necessary for several reasons, and their primary purpose is to treat and restore teeth that have been damaged by dental decay or minor fractures. They are a crucial aspect of restorative dentistry, as they allow dentists to repair and preserve damaged teeth, restore their functionality, and promote overall oral health. Early detection and treatment of dental issues, such as cavities or minor fractures, can prevent more significant dental problems and contribute to maintaining a healthy and functional smile. Regular dental check-ups with Well Beyond Dental and good oral hygiene practices are essential in identifying and addressing dental issues promptly to ensure optimal dental health.

##### Schedule Your Dental Filling Today

We prioritize your oral health and strive to provide exceptional care to all our patients.  Our skilled dental professionals are dedicated to helping you achieve optimal oral health and providing you with a positive dental experience.

Contact us today to book your dental exam and see if a dental filling is necessary. We look forward to partnering with you on your journey to a healthy and beautiful smile.

# Dental Implants

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Restore your smile and confidence with high-quality dental implants at Well Beyond Dental in Reno, NV. Our expert team provides durable, natural-looking tooth replacements designed to improve your oral health, function, and appearance for the long term.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Professional Dental Implants in Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Female-with-Great-Smile.jpg "Professional Dental Implants")

## Dental Tooth Implants in Reno, NV

![Expert Dental Implants in Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Implants.jpg "Expert Dental Implants")

![Dental Implant Services in Reno, NV](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/06/pexels-cottonbro-studio-6502305.jpg "Dental Implant Services")

## What Are Dental Implants?

Dental implants are a permanent, natural-looking solution for replacing missing teeth. They consist of a titanium post that is surgically placed into the jawbone, acting as an artificial tooth root, and a custom crown that mimics the look and function of a real tooth. Unlike dentures or bridges, implants are designed to integrate with your bone, providing exceptional stability and durability.

At Well Beyond Dental in Reno, NV, we offer state-of-the-art dental implant options that restore not only your smile but also your ability to chew, speak, and live with confidence. Whether you’re missing one tooth or multiple teeth, implants are a reliable and long-lasting option for full-mouth restoration.

## Benefits of Dental Implants

Dental implants offer numerous advantages over traditional tooth replacement options. They look, feel, and function like natural teeth, allowing you to eat, speak, and smile with ease. Because implants are anchored into the jawbone, they help prevent bone loss and maintain your facial structure, which is not possible with dentures or bridges alone.

In addition to their durability and aesthetic appeal, implants are low maintenance and can last a lifetime with proper care. Many patients report improved comfort and confidence, as dental implants eliminate the slipping and discomfort often associated with removable dentures.

## The Dental Implant Process

At Well Beyond Dental, we take a personalized approach to your implant journey, ensuring a smooth and comfortable experience. The process begins with a comprehensive consultation, where we evaluate your oral health, review your goals, and create a custom treatment plan. Advanced imaging is used to assess your bone structure and determine the best placement for your implants.

The implant procedure involves placing the titanium post, allowing it time to heal and integrate with your jawbone. Once healing is complete, a custom-made crown is attached, restoring your smile to full strength and beauty. Throughout every step, our Reno dental team is committed to providing expert care and guidance for a successful outcome.
