# Dentures & Partials

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

Here at Well Beyond Dental, we understand the importance of a confident smile and optimal oral health. Our Dentures & Partials service is designed to provide you with personalized solutions to restore your smile and improve your overall well-being. Whether you are missing one tooth or several, our experienced dental team is dedicated to helping you regain function, comfort, and confidence.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Denture & Partials in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Lady-in-dental-chair.jpg "Denture & Partial Services")

## Expert Dentures & Partials in Reno, NV

![Dentures & Partials in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Teeth-Color.jpg "Dentures & Partials")

![Buffing Dental Partial in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Buffing-Dental-Partial.jpg "Dentures & Partials")

##### What are Dentures & Partials?

Dentures and Partials are removable dental appliances designed to replace missing teeth and restore the natural appearance and functionality of your smile. These custom-made prosthetics are crafted to fit comfortably in your mouth, providing a natural and aesthetically pleasing solution for those with missing teeth.

##### Why Dentures and Partials Are Important?

Dentures play a crucial role in restoring functionality to your bite, enabling you to chew food comfortably and speak with confidence. Whether you’re missing a few teeth or require a complete set, our expert team is dedicated to crafting personalized dentures that seamlessly blend with your natural smile. Beyond the functional aspects, dentures contribute significantly to aesthetic enhancement, ensuring that you regain a natural and youthful appearance. We prioritize precision in our denture solutions, striving to create a comfortable and secure fit that allows you to enjoy life without worrying about the limitations often associated with tooth loss.

Dentures and partials at Well Beyond Dental extend far beyond mere prosthetics—they represent a commitment to your overall well-being. Tooth loss can lead to various health issues, such as difficulty in speaking, malnutrition due to compromised chewing ability, and even changes in facial structure. Our comprehensive denture services not only address these concerns but also foster a renewed sense of self-esteem and confidence. We believe that everyone deserves a radiant smile that promotes both physical and emotional health. At Well Beyond Dental, our denture solutions are a testament to our dedication to providing transformative dental care that goes well beyond conventional expectations.

##### Schedule Your Consultation Today

Don’t let missing teeth affect your quality of life. Schedule an appointment with Well Beyond Dental today to explore our Dentures & Partials service. Our team is committed to helping you achieve a healthy, functional, and beautiful smile that goes well beyond your expectations. Contact us now to take the first step towards a brighter, more confident future.

# Tooth Extractions

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental, we understand that tooth extractions can be a daunting prospect for many individuals. However, our compassionate team is dedicated to ensuring a comfortable and stress-free experience for all our patients. Let us guide you through the process and help you achieve optimal oral health.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Tooth Extractions in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/great-smile.jpg "Tooth Extractions in Reno")

## Expert Tooth Extractions in Reno, NV

![WBD Tooth Extractions](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Exam.jpg "WBD Tooth Extractions")

![Dental Extraction](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/patient-in-chair-2.jpg "Dental Extraction")

##### What is a Tooth Extraction & When is it Needed?

**What is a Tooth Extraction?**

A tooth extraction is a dental procedure in which a tooth is removed from its socket in the jawbone. This may be necessary for various reasons, including severe decay, advanced periodontal disease, overcrowding, or trauma to the tooth.

**When is a Tooth Extraction Needed?**

There are several situations in which a tooth extraction may be recommended:

-   **Severe Decay:** When a tooth is extensively decayed and cannot be restored with a filling or crown, extraction may be necessary to prevent the spread of infection to surrounding teeth.
    
-   **Gum Disease:** Advanced periodontal disease can cause loosening of teeth and damage to the surrounding bone, making extraction the best course of action to preserve oral health.
    
-   **Orthodontic Treatment:** In some cases of overcrowding, extraction of one or more teeth may be necessary to create space and achieve proper alignment during orthodontic treatment.
    
-   **Impacted Wisdom Teeth:** Wisdom teeth that are unable to erupt properly due to impaction or misalignment may need to be extracted to prevent pain, infection, and damage to neighboring teeth.
    

##### How Can Tooth Extraction Help?

Although the thought of tooth extraction may seem intimidating, it can provide several benefits:

-   **Relief from Pain:** Extracting a severely damaged or infected tooth can alleviate pain and discomfort, allowing you to enjoy improved oral health and overall well-being.
    
-   **Prevention of Complications:** By removing diseased or impacted teeth, we can prevent further complications such as infection, abscesses, and damage to adjacent teeth.
    
-   **Improved Oral Health:** In many cases, extracting a problematic tooth paves the way for improved oral health and better outcomes for future dental treatments, such as orthodontic procedures or dental implants.
    

##### Why Choose Well Beyond Dental for Tooth Extractions?

When you choose Well Beyond Dental for your tooth extraction needs, you can expect:

-   **Expert Care:** Our experienced dentists and oral surgeons are skilled in performing tooth extractions with precision and care, ensuring optimal results and minimal discomfort.
    
-   **Comfortable Environment:** We prioritize your comfort and well-being throughout the extraction process, offering sedation options and personalized care to ease any anxiety or apprehension.
    
-   **Comprehensive Services:** Whether you require a routine extraction or a more complex procedure, we provide comprehensive dental services tailored to your unique needs, ensuring a seamless experience from start to finish.
    

Don’t let dental issues compromise your quality of life. Schedule an appointment with Well Beyond Dental today and take the first step toward a healthier, happier smile. Your oral health is our top priority, and we’re here to support you every step of the way.

# Periodontal Treatment

[(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

At Well Beyond Dental,  we prioritize your oral health with our comprehensive Periodontal Treatment services. Our team of experienced professionals is dedicated to ensuring that your smile not only looks its best but also stands the test of time. 

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

![Periodontics](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Exam.jpg "Periodontics")

## Professional Periodontist in Reno, NV

![Dentist and Patient](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Lady-in-dental-chair.jpg "Dentist and Patient")

![Periodontal Services](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Dental-Work.jpg "Periodontal Services")

##### What is Periodontal Treatment?

Periodontal Treatment focuses on the health of your gums and supporting structures around your teeth. Periodontal, or gum, diseases can range from mild inflammation (gingivitis) to more severe conditions like periodontitis. Our Periodontal Treatment encompasses a variety of procedures designed to prevent, diagnose, and treat these conditions, ensuring the longevity of your teeth and overall oral health.

##### Why is Periodontal Treatment Important?

Maintaining optimal periodontal health is crucial for overall dental well-being. The gums and supporting structures play a vital role in keeping your teeth secure and healthy.

**Prevention of Gum Diseases:** Regular Periodontal Treatment helps prevent the onset and progression of gum diseases, safeguarding your oral health.

**Preservation of Teeth:** Healthy gums are the foundation for strong and lasting teeth. Periodontal Treatment plays a crucial role in preserving your natural teeth and preventing tooth loss.

**Overall Health Connection:** Research suggests a strong link between oral health and overall well-being. Periodontal diseases have been associated with various systemic conditions, making their prevention and management essential.

**Aesthetic Benefits:** Healthy gums contribute significantly to a beautiful smile. Periodontal Treatment ensures that your gums are in top condition, enhancing the aesthetic appeal of your teeth.

##### Schedule Your Dental Exam Today

Our team consists of skilled and experienced periodontists who specialize in gum health. Rest assured, you’re in the hands of professionals who prioritize precision and excellence.

Don’t wait until gum problems become severe. Take proactive steps towards optimal oral health by scheduling a Periodontal Treatment appointment with Well Beyond Dental. Our team is ready to guide you on the path to a healthy and confident smile.

[Contact us](/contact-us/) today to book your consultation.

# Cold Laser Therapy

At Well Beyond Dental, we offer cold laser therapy to our patients. This is a non-invasive treatment that uses low-level laser energy to help heal the body. It is a safe and effective way to treat a variety of conditions, and can be used on both adults and children.

[Contact Us Today](https://wellbeyonddental.com/our-services/cold-laser-therapy/)

![Cold Laser Therapy](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-Laser.jpg "Cold Laser Therapy")

## Cold Laser Therapy at **Well Beyond Dental**

![Cold Laser Therapy in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Well-Beyond_Still_3.jpg "Cold Laser Therapy in Reno")

![Cold Laser Treatment in Reno](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/gallery_home019-1.jpeg "Cold Laser Treatment in Reno")

##### Cold Laser Therapy

At Well Beyond Dental, a Reno dentist will use cold laser therapy to treat inflammation of the tissues and to heal ulcerations. Cold laser therapy promotes healing of the sensitive area through photobiomodulation (PBM) which is a form of light therapy. Lasers produce different levels of watts, just like lightbulbs, depending on the required usage. We use low-level lasers that use light energy, which is emitted through a handheld wand, to penetrate deep into inflamed muscles. The light is moved over the skin and will produce a warming sensation as it treats the affected tissues. PBM is very effective after longer dental treatments or if you have inflammation in your mouth or jaw joints. This type of laser therapy will pass right through healthy cells and only affects damaged cells making this process completely safe and painless.

##### What it Does

Cold laser therapy, also called [MLS laser therapy](https://www.bioresearchinc.com/products/mls), works using wavelengths of low-level light that are applied directly to the affected area. The tissue absorbs the light and causes a reaction where damaged cells respond and begin regenerating. This innovative technology allows our Reno dentists to treat migraines and headaches, joint inflammation, sensitive teeth, fever blisters or cold sores, and surgical or trauma sites. This method of treatment promotes faster wound healing and improved nerve function along with accelerated tissue repair and cell growth.

If you’re suffering from any of the following concerns, cold laser therapy may be an effective treatment method for you:

-   Grinding teeth
-   Pain
-   Clenching
-   Headaches

##### Benefits

Cold laser therapy is a non-invasive, non-surgical procedure that is completely safe and painless. This procedure works using two wavelengths – one that reduces inflammation and one that relieves pain – to produce consistent and repeatable results. There are many benefits to using cold laser therapy treatments, such as:

-   Speeds the healing of soft tissue damage
-   Reduces the duration and frequency of lesions such as cold sores
-   Reduces pain after dental treatments
-   Pain management with no negative side effects
-   Immediate relief by reducing muscle inflammation
-   Treats the cause of tense muscles related to TMJ
-   Each procedure is quick, usually lasting under 10 minutes
-   Promotes tissue regeneration and healing by increasing oxygen and blood flow to the affected tissues

## Your Journey To A Beautiful Smile Starts  
With Invisalign Aligners at Well Beyond Dental

[Book Online](https://onlinebooking.mydentistlink.com/6645e5fa-f064-47c0-acaa-1e4559ea5132/691e6c94-e3f1-403c-aa8d-99da0476ac39)

Reno, NV 89523



![Invisalign](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/Invisilign-01.webp "Invisalign")

## Invest in Your Smile Today With This Extended Offer!

If you’re searching for Invisalign near me, our clear aligner solutions are designed to gradually and effectively straighten your teeth while fitting seamlessly into your lifestyle. Our Invisalign Clear Aligners in Reno, NV are a modern orthodontic treatment that uses custom-fit, removable clear aligners to provide a discreet and comfortable way to achieve a perfectly aligned smile.

At Well Beyond Dental, a dedicated Smile Specialist will begin by performing a precise digital scan of your teeth. This cutting-edge technology allows us to partner with Invisalign to create a personalized treatment plan tailored to your unique needs. From your first aligner to your final Invisalign retainer, the process ensures consistent progress, gently moving your teeth into their ideal positions. With Invisalign, you’ll enjoy a beautifully straight smile without the inconvenience of traditional braces.

[Contact Us Today For A Free Consultation](tel:7757863472)



### Experienced

We’ve partnered with Invisalign, a company with years of experience in providing orthodontic treatment solutions.



### Endorsed

Invisalign clear aligners are backed by a sophisticated dental laboratory known for its advancements in dental technologies.



### Customized

Invisalign treatment plans are customized to tailor to your specific needs so that you can feel confident in achieving the results you want.

![Invisalign Clear Aligners](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Dental-X-Ray.jpg)

#### Step 01 - Initial Appointment

It all starts with a digital scan. No Need for Messy Impressions, everything is done using digital scans.

![Customized Treatment Plan](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Dental-Analysis.jpg)

#### Step 02 - Customized Treatment Plan

We Work With Invisalign experts work together to develop a customized treatment plan just for you. On average, plan for about 2-4 weeks from your initial appointment to receive your aligners.

![Start Treatment](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/3-e1715610410153.jpg)

#### Step 03 - Start Treatment

Return to your doctor to receive your new patient welcome kit, Invisalign Clear Aligners. Expect to wear your aligners for two weeks or as prescribed by your doctor. Your doctor will tell you how frequently to change your aligners and how many hours per day to wear them.

![Ongoing Check-Ins](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/Happy-Dental-Patient.jpg)

#### Step 04 - Ongoing Check-Ins

It all starts with a digital scan. No Need for Messy Impressions, everything is done using digital scans.

## The Smile of Your Dreams in  
**Just One Day**

At Well Beyond Dental, we are passionate about providing you with a reason to smile confidently. Using cutting-edge technology like digital smile design and 3D printing, we offer a fast new smile with everything conveniently done in-house on the same day. Our one day smile solution ensures you leave with the results you’ve always dreamed of without the wait.

Specializing in transforming lives one smile at a time, we focus on personalized care that meets your unique needs. Whether you’re looking for a same day dental solution or a beautifully customized design, Well Beyond Dental is here to help you achieve a radiant smile that truly shines.

[Contact Us Today For A Consultation](tel:7757863472)

![Same Day Smile Design](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/SDSD_LOGO_SUN_BIG@8x.webp "Same Day Smile Design")

## THE Same Day Smile  
Design Process

![Same Day Smile Consultation](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/Same-Day-Smile-Consultation.jpg)

#### Consultation

Begin your journey with a detailed consultation where our dental experts will understand your goals and assess your oral health.

1

![Same Day Smile Consultation](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/WBD-X-Ray.jpg)

#### Digital Imaging

Utilizing advanced digital imaging, we create a virtual blueprint of your new smile, allowing for precise planning and customization.

2

#### Treatment Plan

Based on your preferences and the digital design, our team develops a comprehensive treatment plan tailored to your individual needs.

3

![Same Day Smile Consultation](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/10/Same-Day-Smile-Application.jpg)

#### Same Day Application

Witness the magic unfold as our skilled dentists apply the planned enhancements in a single visit, ensuring immediate results and minimal disruption to your schedule.

4

Efficiency

Our streamlined process allows you to achieve your dream smile without the wait. No need for multiple visits; witness the transformation in just one appointment. Our dental experts leverage the latest innovations for optimal outcomes.

Same-day smile design isn’t just about aesthetics; it’s about creating a healthy and functional smile. Our approach addresses both cosmetic and oral health aspects, ensuring your new smile is not only beautiful but also long-lasting.

![Before-After1](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Before-After1.jpg "Before-After1")

Because Your Skin Is Worth It

# Laser Skin Rejuvenation

![](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/beauty-esthetician-38.png "beauty-esthetician-38")

#### CoolPeel CO2

[![Laser CoolPeel B:A.jpg](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-CoolPeel-BA.jpg.jpg "Laser CoolPeel B:A.jpg")](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-CoolPeel-BA.jpg.jpg "Laser CoolPeel B:A.jpg")

#### CoolPeel CO₂

[![Laser Tetra B:A](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-Tetra-BA.jpg "Laser Tetra B:A")](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-Tetra-BA.jpg "Laser Tetra B:A")

#### Everesse Skin Tightening

[![Laser Everesse B:A.jpg](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-Everesse-BA.jpg.jpg "Laser Everesse B:A.jpg")](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/Laser-Everesse-BA.jpg.jpg "Laser Everesse B:A.jpg")

Advanced Skin Resurfacing

## Reveal Your Best Skin with CoolPeel CO2

**CoolPeel CO2 is an advanced resurfacing treatment that refreshes the skin with significantly less heat than traditional CO2 lasers.** It gently targets the outermost layer of the skin to improve texture, soften fine lines, minimize pores, and reduce visible sun damage. Because the treatment is designed to avoid unnecessary thermal injury, downtime is minimal. Most patients experience one to two days of redness and a temporary dry or rough feel as the skin heals. Hydration is important during this period and helps reveal smoother, brighter, more refined skin. CoolPeel CO2 is ideal for anyone looking for noticeable rejuvenation without an extended recovery period.

[Learn More](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

Next Generation Skin Tightening

## Everesse Skin Tightening

**Everesse is a next generation skin tightening treatment that uses monopolar radiofrequency to lift and firm the face, neck, and eye area without surgery or downtime.** The energy penetrates deep beneath the skin to stimulate collagen and elastin production, restoring youthful volume and definition. Everesse is safe for all skin types and offers a comfortable experience that fits easily into a busy schedule. Many patients notice a subtle lift right away, and results continue to improve for up to six months as new collagen develops. It is a powerful option for those seeking tighter contours and long lasting rejuvenation without invasive procedures.

[Learn More](https://wellbeyonddental.com/contact-us/)

# **Well Beyond Dental Privacy Policy**

This Privacy Policy describes how your personal information is collected, used, and shared when you visit or make a purchase from [www.wellbeyonddental.com](https://www.wellbeyonddental.com/) (the “Site”).

## **PERSONAL INFORMATION WE COLLECT**

When you visit the Site, we automatically collect certain information about your device, including information about your web browser, IP address, time zone, and some of the cookies that are installed on your device. Additionally, as you browse the Site, we collect information about the individual web pages or products that you view, what websites or search terms referred you to the Site, and information about how you interact with the Site. We refer to this automatically-collected information as “Device Information.”

### **We collect Device Information using the following technologies:**

– “Cookies” are data files that are placed on your device or computer and often include an anonymous unique identifier. For more information about cookies, and how to disable cookies, visit [https://www.allaboutcookies.org](https://www.allaboutcookies.org/).

– “Log files” track actions occurring on the Site, and collect data including your IP address, browser type, Internet service provider, referring/exit pages, and date/time stamps.

– “Web beacons,” “tags,” and “pixels” are electronic files used to record information about how you browse the Site.

Additionally when you make a purchase or attempt to make a purchase through the Site, we collect certain information from you, including your name, billing address, shipping address, payment information (including credit card numbers), email address, and phone number.  We refer to this information as “Order Information.”

When we talk about “Personal Information” in this Privacy Policy, we are talking both about Device Information and Order Information.

## **HOW DO WE USE YOUR PERSONAL INFORMATION?**

We use the Order Information that we collect generally to fulfill any orders placed through the Site (including processing your payment information, arranging for shipping, and providing you with invoices and/or order confirmations).  Additionally, we use this Order Information to:

Communicate with you;

Screen our orders for potential risk or fraud; and

When in line with the preferences you have shared with us, provide you with information or advertising relating to our products or services.

We use the Device Information that we collect to help us screen for potential risk and fraud (in particular, your IP address), and more generally to improve and optimize our Site (for example, by generating analytics about how our customers browse and interact with the Site, and to assess the success of our marketing and advertising campaigns).

### **SHARING YOUR PERSONAL INFORMATION**

We share your Personal Information with third parties to help us use your Personal Information, as described above.   We also use Google Analytics to help us understand how our customers use the Site–you can read more about how Google uses your Personal Information here:  [https://www.google.com/intl/en/policies/privacy/](https://www.google.com/intl/en/policies/privacy/).  You can also opt-out of Google Analytics here:  [https://tools.google.com/dlpage/gaoptout](https://tools.google.com/dlpage/gaoptout).

Finally, we may also share your Personal Information to comply with applicable laws and regulations, to respond to a subpoena, search warrant or other lawful request for information we receive, or to otherwise protect our rights.

As described above, we use your Personal Information to provide you with targeted advertisements or marketing communications we believe may be of interest to you.  For more information about how targeted advertising works, you can visit the Network Advertising Initiative’s (“NAI”) educational page at [www.networkadvertising.org/understanding-online-advertising/how-does-it-work](https://www.networkadvertising.org/understanding-online-advertising/how-does-it-work).

You can opt out of targeted advertising by:

FACEBOOK – [www.facebook.com/settings/?tab=ads](https://www.facebook.com/settings/?tab=ads)  
GOOGLE – [www.google.com/settings/ads/anonymous](https://www.google.com/settings/ads/anonymous)  
BING – [https://advertise.bingads.microsoft.com/en-us/resources/policies/personalized-ads](https://advertise.bingads.microsoft.com/en-us/resources/policies/personalized-ads)

Additionally, you can opt out of some of these services by visiting the Digital Advertising Alliance’s opt-out portal at:  [https://www.optout.aboutads.info/](https://optout.aboutads.info/).

### **DO NOT TRACK**

Please note that we do not alter our Site’s data collection and use practices when we see a Do Not Track signal from your browser.

If you are a European resident, you have the right to access personal information we hold about you and to ask that your personal information be corrected, updated, or deleted. If you would like to exercise this right, please contact us through the contact information below.

Additionally, if you are a European resident we note that we are processing your information in order to fulfill contracts we might have with you (for example if you make an order through the Site), or otherwise to pursue our legitimate business interests listed above.  Additionally, please note that your information will be transferred outside of Europe, including to Canada and the United States.

When you place an order through the Site, we will maintain your Order Information for our records unless and until you ask us to delete this information.

### **CHANGES**

We may update this privacy policy from time to time in order to reflect, for example, changes to our practices or for other operational, legal or regulatory reasons.

### **CONTACT US**

For more information about our privacy practices, if you have questions, or if you would like to make a complaint, please contact us by phone at [(775) 786-3472](tel:7757863472)

![Dr. Tammy Neugebauer](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/Dr.Tammy_.jpeg "Dr. Tammy Neugebauer")

# Dr. Tammy Neugebauer

## DDS

Born and raised in Reno, Dr. Tammy attended the University of Nevada for her undergraduate education. Both her dad, Monte Neugebauer, and brother, Brady Neugebauer, are dentists and because of their influence Dr. Tammy made the career choice to attend University of the Pacific, Dugoni School of Dentistry in San Francisco. After graduating in 2005, her education continued in Florida where she attended an Academic Education in General Dentistry at Nova Southeastern University. Dr. Tammy returned to Reno in 2006 and began practicing at Dr. Lloyd B Austin’s pediatric dental office as well as an associate with her family at Reno Dental Associates. In 2013, Dr. Tammy took ownership of Dr. Monte’s practice allowing him to slow down and eventually retire in 2016.

After so many years treating patients’ dental problems, Dr. Tammy always had a thought that there was an underlying problem contributing to the symptoms she was treating daily. She was fortunate to attend a lecture by Dr. Steve Olmos, DDS who discussed how the origin of symptoms was often related to sleep, breathing and pain. Dr. Tammy, suffering from these symptoms herself had found the missing link in how she was treating her patients. Dr. Tammy started screening patients with just a few simple questions. Can you get to sleep, stay asleep, and do you feel rested in the morning? Do you have headaches? It was astounding how many of her patients had at least one “yes” to these questions. Dr. Tammy felt she needed to help her patients, so she continued learning to better educate herself so she could be a better provider for her patients. Sleep, breathing and pain became her passion and mission to help as many people suffering from these underlying, under-treated problems.

Dr. Tammy joined forces with Dr. Olmos partnering as the TMJ Sleep Therapy Centre of Reno. Dr. Tammy continues improving her knowledge and experience so she can better serve her patients. With the growth of her practice and continued development of her skills, Dr. Tammy needed more space and more help. Her husband joined her practice in 2016 to help manage and create systems that have made her practice go above and beyond. They realized they needed more space and technology in order to achieve this. On February 1, 2019, Dr. Tammy and Brian proudly opened their new state-of-the-art dental practice, Well Beyond Dental and the TMJ sleep Therapy Centre of Reno.

![Dr. Verano](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Dr.-Verano.jpg "Dr. Verano")

# Dr. Mariangela Verano

## DDS

Dr. Mariangela Verano is an experienced dentist with more than 25 years in practice. A native of the San Francisco Bay Area, she moved to Reno in 2019. She earned her Bachelor of Science from UCLA, followed by her Doctor of Dental Surgery degree from UCSF. She also completed an Advanced Education in General Dentistry residency at UCSF, reflecting her strong commitment to continued education and clinical growth.

She is especially dedicated to patient education and whole-body wellness. Dr. Verano believes in a complete health approach to dentistry, with a strong focus on the relationship between oral health, overall health, and airway function. Her interest in this area led her to become a Breathe Institute Ambassador, and she is now beginning a residency that will position her as Nevada’s first Breathe Institute affiliate. She also founded the Northern Nevada Breathe Collaboration group, where she works alongside other providers to improve care for patients dealing with sleep-disordered breathing and related health concerns.

Dr. Verano is known for her careful diagnostic process and thorough treatment planning. Her clinical interests include sleep apnea, snoring, mouth breathing, and other airway-related conditions. She takes a functional, team-based approach to care and places special importance on healthy oral and facial development in children.

Her dedication to patients is reflected in both her detailed approach and strong work ethic. She remains active in several professional organizations, including the American Academy for Oral Systemic Health, the American Academy of Physiological Medicine and Dentistry, The Breathe Institute, Airway Circle, Airway Health Solutions, the Nevada Dental Association, and the American Dental Association. When she is away from the office, Dr. Verano enjoys golfing, skiing, traveling, and spending time with family and friends.

# Dr. Allison Lundgren

## DDS

Born and raised in northwest Reno, Dr. Allison Lundgren has always had a passion for both science and helping people. Growing up, she has always wanted to be a dentist, influenced by her own positive experiences at the dentist. After earning a Bachelor of Science in Biomedical Engineering from the University of Nevada, Reno, Dr. Lundgren attended the University of Nevada, Las Vegas School of Dental Medicine (but don’t worry, she proudly bleeds blue). She spent a year practicing in Northern Colorado before returning to Reno to be closer to her family and friends. Dr. Lundgren is an avid lifelong learner and committed to staying up to date with the latest advancements in the dynamic field of dentistry. Her commitment to continuing education ensures she provides the highest quality care to her patients. She strives to give everyone a positive dental experience. Outside of the office, Dr. Lundgren loves exploring the outdoors, often hiking with her dog, Trapper. She enjoys spending quality time with her loved ones, watching football, baking, and taking full advantage of all that Northern Nevada has to offer. Dr. Lundgren is thrilled to be home again and excited about serving Reno and the surrounding communities. She looks forward to meeting you soon!

# Dr. Brandon Morales

## DDS

Dr. Brandon J. Morales has spent most of his life in Northern Nevada but has lived all over the country during his lifetime. His ultimate goal is to ensure that all his patients experience the upmost comfort and comprehensive care under his hands and to build long lasting relationships with everyone he treats. He is a graduate from the University of Nevada, Reno where he received his Bachelors in Biology and went on to receive his Doctorate of Dental Medicine from the University of Nevada, Las Vegas. Dr. Morales is an active member of many professional organizations including:

Academy of General Dentistry

American Dental Association

Nevada Dental Association

Northern Nevada Dental Association

Dr. Morales prides himself on providing the highest standard of care for all the patients he treats and enjoys all aspects of Dentistry. He is an avid outdoorsman and when he’s not caring for his patients he enjoys mountain biking, skiing, camping, traveling, and all other types of outdoor activities. Dr. Morales and his wife Ari welcomed their first baby, a little girl named Max, into the world in the fall of 2025.

![Brian Wiltshire](https://wellbeyonddental.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Brian-Wiltshire.jpg "Brian Wiltshire")

# Brian

## Chief Operations Officer

Brian grew up in Reno and received his BS in Biology from the University of Nevada in 2006. He decided to expand his studies the next year starting the MBA program at the University of Nevada. During the MBA program, Brian worked in logistics, focusing on process improvements and employee management. Brian and Tammy had their daughter, Kaila, in 2009. Shortly after, he decided to make a career shift and began law school at the University of the Pacific, McGeorge School of Law in 2011. Two weeks before law school started, Tammy gave birth to their son Shaun. Brian commuted back and forth from Reno to Sacramento for three years to complete his law degree (that was a crazy three years!!).

After graduating, Brian passed the Nevada Bar and was accepted into the Nevada Bar Association. Brian began working for Chief Judge Michael Gibbons as one of the Nevada Court of Appeal’s first law clerks. When the clerkship was over, he decided to apply his business acumen to Tammy’s practice and started to manage it in 2016. Having a legal and business background allows him to create more efficient systems, understand and educate patients on their rights and responsibilities according to their insurance plans, and navigate challenges brought by ever-changing healthcare rules and regulations.

Brian has been with the practice for over four years and was integral in relocating the practice to its current location to accommodate Reno’s growth and the expansion of Tammy’s dentistry practice in Reno. Of the many qualities he loves about working at Well Beyond Dental, the most important to him is the culture. “Each member of our team believes in making improvements every day to provide care that surpasses our patients’ expectations. Every team member delivers such exceptional and personalized care that it is no wonder our patients put their health in our hands. I invite everyone to experience how our approach is different and see for yourself how our team is committed to providing care that is well beyond dental.”
